| `account.rs` *(launch A1; 1.178.0 A3/A5)* | The Plyloft account, **optional since 1.178.0**: nothing needs it except a team. GitHub OAuth Device Flow (public client id, no scopes) → `POST /v1/session` (the GitHub token is sent once and dropped) → session token in the OS keychain (`keyring`, service `com.mbadv.ply.account`, keyed by `deviceId`; debug builds use `com.mbadv.ply.account.dev` and `account.dev.json`, since `tauri dev` shares the installed app's config dir) → `PLY2-` entitlement verified offline (Ed25519 `verify_strict`, `v == 2`, own `deviceId`, `now < exp`) and cached in `<config_dir>/account.json`. Refresh at launch, every 6 h (wall clock, 30 min after an offline try) and on demand; `unauthorized` drops to signed-out Free (the toast "Signed out of Plyloft on this Mac" comes from the store, only on a computer that follows a team); a network failure keeps the cached entitlement until `exp` (`offline`). **Only Team is "one computer at a time".** Every call sends `X-Plyloft-Team-Choice` (`TEAM_CHOICE_HEADER`); a server that echoes it keeps every computer signed in and signs `plan: "free"` for a computer where the account's Team plan or seat is not active, saying where it is in the **unsigned** `team` field (`{active: here|elsewhere|nowhere, teamRepo, teamRole, device}`), which the app maps to `AccountInfo.team` (`AccountTeam {state, device, teamRepo, role}`, `device` the generic label such as "MacBook Pro", never the computer's own name) — shown only, never a gate. `account_use_team` (`AccountStore::use_team_at`, `POST /session/use-team`) makes this computer the one Team runs on; the other stays signed in, on Free. `AccountInfo.last_known_plan` keeps the plan of the last verified entitlement, also once it expired or went: the offline and "plan not checked" notices show only when it was Team. A `replaced` answer is no longer a state anybody sees (`account_reclaim` and the "Your plan is on … · Use it here" callout are gone): the entitlement goes, and from a server that echoed the header the session is taken back silently with `POST /session/reclaim` (at most once per offline retry; `reclaimable: false` or an `unauthorized` reclaim means the server closed the session, signed out quietly; any other failure leaves the account signed in on Free until the next try); from an older server (no echo, a rollback) there is no reclaim and the account stays signed in on Free (`AccountStore::on_replaced`). Every change emits `account://changed`. `AccountInfo` is the policy source for the plan gates (G1–G3). HTTP and keychain are traits, so tests use no network and no keychain. Launch A3: the main window regaining focus refreshes too, at most once a minute counted from the last refresh of any kind, stamped when a refresh starts (`refresh_on_focus`, `claim_focus_refresh`; `lib.rs`'s `WindowEvent::Focused`); the refresher defers to an ask made under a minute ago (`refresher_step`). Contract: `docs/plans/LAUNCH_API.md`. |
| `billing_client.rs` *(launch T1–T3, A3; 1.178.0)* | `POST /checkout` (**Team only**: Pro is no longer sold, and the server answers `410 plan_retired` to an old app that asks for it), `POST /portal`, `GET`/`POST /team` and the seat calls over `AccountStore::api_send` (session from the keychain, `X-Plyloft-Device`, `X-Plyloft-Team-Choice`; `unauthorized` ends the session); https-only links; typed `already_subscribed` / `already_bound` / `not_found`. Commands `account_checkout` (accepts only Team: any other plan is refused with "Only the Team plan has a checkout."), `account_portal` open the page in the browser. The Pro → Team switch (`switch_to_team`, `switch_preview`, `team_switch_*`) is removed with Pro; `AccountError.url` and `is_invoice_page` remain, unused until the server work drops them. `account_sign_out_others` → `POST /sessions/others/sign-out`. |
| `license.rs` *(v1.85.0 — team tier cycle 2; launch G1–G3; 1.178.0)* | Where every plan gate asks. Since launch G1–G3 (1.158.0) the plan comes from the Plyloft account (`AccountInfo`, a verified `PLY2` entitlement), not from a key; `LicenseStore` keeps its name so the gate call sites stay put. **Since 1.178.0 there are two plans, Free and Team: everything for one person runs on every plan, there is no Pro gate (`pro_allowed`, `pro_gate`, `ProFeature`, `release_plan_gate`) and no Free card limit (`plan_gates.rs`, `start_check.rs` are deleted).** `Plan::Pro` stays readable in an old entitlement and counts as Free. `PlanGates::of` answers all at once (`plan_gates_get` for the frontend, which never re-derives): **Team** (`team_access` → `TeamAccess`: `Allowed` for plan `team` with a valid entitlement, `teamRepo` equal to `settings.team.teamRepo` ignoring case, and the Plyloft login equal to `settings.team.account`; otherwise `NoTeamPlan`, `SignedOut` (not signed in to Plyloft), `TeamElsewhere` (the account's Team plan or seat runs on another computer — the unsigned `team` field only picks these words; the gate is closed because the signed plan isn't Team), `TeamToChoose` (Team is ready on no computer yet, `team.state` `nowhere`), `NoTeam`, `OtherRepo`, `OtherAccount`, `NoSeat`; the leads `Team plan needed:`, `Plyloft account needed:`, `Team is on another computer:`, `Choose a computer to use Team.`, `Your Team plan is for another team:`, `Signed in as another account:`, mirrored in `src/lib/planGates.ts` and pinned by `the_frontend_knows_every_lead`). `TEAM_FREE_DURING_BETA` is gone. `commands.rs::team_gate` still enforces Team on GH-backing/claims, shared-library writes, overlap warnings and the pull requests a teammate asks you to review (`team_allowed`, `inbox_eligibility.rs`), at action points only (a lapse never aborts running work; a GitHub board degrades to read-only with Switch to Local). The `PLY1` key (`PLY1-<b64url(payload)>.<b64url(sig)>`, `{team, seats, issuedAt, expiresAt, teamRepo?}`) is retired: a stored key unlocks nothing, `license.json` is left untouched and only read for Diagnostics (`license_get`; `license_set`/`license_clear` removed), and `adopt_key_team_once` gives a key's team repo to an empty `settings.team.teamRepo` once per profile (marker `license-team-adopted`). |
| `pr_review.rs` *(Challenger on a pull request)* | Board › ⋯ › Review a pull request (QoL 1.168 block 10: moved from the header). `PrReview` on the card (`prReview`: number, repo, base/head branch, the head GitHub last reported, `branchHead` = the head the card's branch was last brought to by an admitted review, a push or Bring in the new commits — the head a fix is built on, `heads` = the card's snapshots that are pull request heads, `pushable` + `fixNote`, state, `checkedAt`, `readError`, `challenger` = the vendor chosen in the sheet, which reviews whatever vendor builds, `slotReleased`). `create` (IPC `pr_review_create`): parses a link or number of the workspace's GitHub repository (from origin), reads it with `gh pr view` as the workspace's account, refuses a merged/closed one, returns the card that already reviews it, decides `fix_permission` (same repository, opened by the member, GitHub doesn't refuse their push, head ≠ base), fetches `refs/pull/<n>/head` into `refs/ply/pr/<n>` (`worktrees::fetch_pull_head`), then under `CARD_START_LOCK` asks the Free slot, makes the card In progress (never a GitHub issue) with its worktree on a card branch at that head (`worktrees::ensure_for_pr_card`), a recorded base where the head meets `origin/<base>`, a Builder state with no session, and starts the review (`builder::review_pr_head`) under the same lock. `start` (Review / Review again) reads it again, fetches its head, brings the branch there (`worktrees::move_pr_card_branch`: fast-forward, or a reset when the branch held nothing of its own; `MERGE_FIRST` when an unpushed fix has new commits on top; refused with edits or while a Builder session is open), re-records the base if the pull request was retargeted, asks the slot (`ask_slot`) and reviews it. `merge_new_commits` (IPC `pr_review_merge_head`) merges new commits under an unpushed fix (`worktrees::merge_pr_head_into_card`). The Free slot (owner decision B): `holds_slot` = reviewing or a fix not pushed, open and not `slotReleased`; a Builder at work holds it through `plan_gates`' back-at-work claim; slot holders are read again before a refusal (`refresh_slot_holders`). `refresh_card` and its own poller (`start_poller`: every `POLL_MS` per card, `READS_PER_TICK` a tick, a global pause on a rate limit) keep `head_sha`/state; merged or closed, `clean_up_closed` moves the worktree to the trash (`pr_status::clean_up_card_worktree`, N18's branch record) and deletes `refs/ply/pr/<n>`. `review_offer` / `inbox_ask` say what the card offers and asks (`inbox.rs`). `review_target` (the pull request's base) is the review's `target_branch` in `reviewer::run_review` and `card_review_diff`. `builder_refusal` holds every Builder start on anyone else's pull request; `work_refusal` Verify and line comments; `ship_refusal` Ship, Ship batch and Merge; `PUT_BACK_REFUSAL` put back. Push the fixes is `card_pr_push_update` (owner decision A, AGENTS.md): `pr_fix::push_update` sends the verified snapshot to the pull request's own branch with `--force-with-lease` on `branchHead`, only while GitHub's `headRefOid` is it and the fix contains it, never to the workspace target, the default branch or the base (`protected_branch_refusal`); its `after_push` records the head and posts nothing. Nothing here writes to GitHub. Tests in `pr_review/tests.rs` (with a mock-app flow). |
| `pr_auto_fix.rs` *(G7 1.170.0; Rust gate 1.174.0 B6, CH-3)* | "Fix failing checks by itself": the record on the pull request (`PrStatus.autoFix`: `heads` tried, one per head, at most `MAX_ATTEMPTS` = 3; `stopped`; `startedAt` of the latest try, 1.174.0), `begin`/`stop`/`set_switch`, the Inbox stop. The window drives the loop (`lib/prAutoFix.ts`, `store/prAutoFixStore.ts`); its push is `card_pr_push_update` with `auto: true`, which `pr_fix::push_update_auto` refuses (nothing pushed, Verify never started) unless `auto_push_refusal` finds every G7 condition (switch on, not a pull request review card, checks failing on the head a running try began for, within three tries, not stopped, the latest snapshot finished after the try began and approved by the Challenger or its review skipped) and `ship_decision` reads that snapshot as the verified commit. The person's *Send the fixes to GitHub* sends no `auto`. |
| `team_billing.rs` *(launch T1–T3, D9, D17)* | A team with seats and payment. **T1:** `create_stage(account, team)` says where Create team stands (`SignIn`, `Choose`, `Create {comp}`, `Bound`, `Member`, `Lapsed`; the Pro → Team stage `HasPro` is gone); the repository is created only once the entitlement says `team`/owner with no repo, then bound (`bind_created_team` → `POST /team`), so an abandoned Checkout leaves nothing; `wait_for_team_plan` polls up to two minutes after Checkout. **T2:** `add_member_with_seat` takes the seat on the server (by GitHub id) **before** anything is written or granted on GitHub; `free_seat_after_removal` frees it once GitHub access is gone. **T3** (a member with no seat is told whom to ask) lives in the gates and on the Team page. |
| `inbox.rs` *(v1.65.0 — UX revamp step 1; hardened v1.102.0)* | The cross-workspace Inbox (docs/ux-revamp/STEP1.md). **The feed is a VIEW** — `assemble_feed` derives events from state already persisted (cards' run/verify/ship histories via the managed `KanbanStore` — cold workspaces included; an mtime-windowed `AgentRegistry::list_runs_modified_since` scan; TTL-cached (5 min) `gh` review-requested PRs). Event ids are stable (run ids / commit SHAs / PR numbers — never `updated_at`); dedup suppresses swarm legs + card-linked runs. Needs-you/failed card events are NOT windowed (unresolved still wants you); shipped + standalone-run events age out at `INBOX_WINDOW_DAYS = 14`. `rollup_costs` buckets `total_cost_usd` into 4 weekly buckets per workspace (Inbox footer). `SeenStore` over `<config_dir>/inbox/seen.json` — the ONLY new persisted state: per-user (`identity.userEmail` \| `"local"`) `eventId → seenAt` maps, atomic writes, self-pruning at 2× window. v1.102.0 adds a strict non-repairing read path, a redacted canonical eligible-set proof, PR-cache provenance, and mutex-owned compare-before-write for bulk seen mutation. seen ≠ resolved: resolved is never stored — acting mutates the source store and the row leaves the feed on the next derivation. |
| `codeowners.rs` *(v1.62.0 — Phase 6)* | Pure: `parse(text) -> Vec<Rule>` + `path_matches(pattern, rel_path)` (minimal gitignore-style: `/`-anchors, trailing-`/` dir prefixes, `*` single-segment + `**` cross-segment globs) + `flagged_owners(text, rel_path)` with **last-match-wins** semantics (a no-owner rule clears ownership). Read by the `codeowners_check` IPC to drive the editor ribbon. "Covers our CODEOWNERS patterns, not the full spec." 9 unit tests. |
| `snapshots.rs` *(v0.9.0 F1)* | `SnapshotsStore` over `<config_dir>/snapshots/<runId>/`. `capture_before(runId, cwd)` walks the cwd respecting noisy-dir excludes and copies qualifying files (text only, ≤100KB, depth ≤12, count ≤500) to `snapshots/<runId>/before/<rel-path>`. `compute_changes(runId, cwd, ...)` re-walks cwd, diffs against `before/`, builds `ChangesManifest { changes: Vec<FileChange { path, kind, before, after }> }`, writes atomically to `snapshots/<runId>/changes.json`, then deletes `before/`. `read_changes(runId)` returns the persisted manifest or `None`. 5 unit tests cover modified/added/removed detection, noisy-dir + dotfile + binary-file skips, idempotent re-read, missing-manifest case. **v1.10.2 (Cycle A)**: when the run's `project_cwd` is a per-card worktree (`<workspace_cwd>/.plyloft-worktrees/<card_id>/`, or the legacy `.atelier-worktrees/`), `capture_before` automatically reroots there — no code change needed in this module. |
| `worktrees.rs` *(v1.10.0 / v1.11.0 — Cycle A + B)* | Stateless module for per-card ephemeral git worktrees. `ensure_for_card(workspace_cwd, card_id, title, target_branch)` runs `git worktree add` at `<workspace_cwd>/.plyloft-worktrees/<card_id>/` on branch `plyloft/card-<short_id>-<slug>` (idempotent on re-call; rejects path/branch collisions). `<short_id>` (`branch_short_id`) is the first eight alphanumerics of the card id after a `card-` / `card_` prefix; **1.175.0 (TM-2):** for a GitHub Issues card (`gh-issue-<N>`) it is `issue<N>`, the whole number, since `ghissue` plus the number's first digit was shared by every issue with that digit, and two with one title made one branch. `earlier_branch_names` gives the `ghissue<d>-<slug>` names such a card had before: `ensure_for_card` keeps the card's own worktree folder on one and never refuses a branch by that name as this card's leftover (it may be another issue's); a card starting again picks one up only when the card was started before and no other card of the board records it or, started before too, would have made it (`branch_cleanup::restart_candidates`, asked through `restart_candidates_on` by the start); the Inbox's old-branch scan knows them and takes a name two cards share for neither's. **N16:** `WORKTREES_DIR` (`.plyloft-worktrees`) is where new worktrees go; `LEGACY_WORKTREES_DIRS` (`.atelier-worktrees`) is where older cards keep theirs, never moved (Claude Code and Codex key session history on the cwd). `worktree_path_for_card` finds an existing worktree under either root (current first), `card_worktree_path` prefers the path the card stores, `worktree_roots` / `is_under_worktree_root` (the reveal guard; `..` refused) cover both, and the root ignores itself through its own `.gitignore` (`*`) — the user's `.gitignore` is never written. `prune_for_card` renames the dir into the `.trash/<card_id>-<unix_ms>/` of the root it lives in + `git worktree prune`; the branch is preserved — `gc_trash` deletes no branch. **N18:** Ship, Land, a merged pull request (`prune_pr_merged_card`), Sync's merged prune (neither deletes the branch itself any more) and an archive record the card's branch, its tip and the trash entry on the card (`KanbanCard::branch_cleanup`); `branch_cleanup.rs` sweeps them right after `gc_trash` in `build_cache::gc_workspace` and deletes a branch with `update-ref -d <ref> <recorded tip>` only once its 7 days are up and its trash entry is gone, for this Mac's own cards, never while checked out anywhere or in a session, and only with the workspace's `keepCardBranches` off; an archived card's branch waits for the Inbox ask, and the one-time backlog (`Workspace::branch_backlog`) offers branches of cards shipped before it. `commit_pending_in_worktree` flushes uncommitted edits in a worktree before a squash-merge (used by `ship.rs`). `squash_merge_into_workspace` runs `git merge --squash <branch>` from the workspace root. `branch_is_merged_into` for safety checks. `merged_into_origin` (branch has its own commits, all in the fetched `origin/<target>`) drives `prune_merged_card_worktrees`, the reconcile/Sync pass that trashes clean merged card worktrees and (N18) reports the proven tips for the branch record instead of deleting the branches, and `overlap::pending_files`. `gc_trash(cwd, cache_paths) -> TrashGc` reads every root's `.trash/`, reaps entries older than `TRASH_TTL_SECS` (7 days) and strips build caches from the rest by the card index each prune keeps beside its entry (`.trash/<entry>.index`; no copy → untouched; each entry keeps its mtime) — run by `build_cache::spawn_trash_gc` at launch and daily, and after every GitHub reconcile/Sync; it ends by removing the orphaned legacy `.cargo-target` and `.cargo-target.clearing-*` at once (`drop_legacy_shared_targets`, real directories only, under its own lock), then `retire_legacy_roots`, which removes a legacy root holding nothing live (only an empty `.trash/`, its own `.gitignore`, those caches and OS litter — `.DS_Store`, `Thumbs.db`, `desktop.ini`, any case; anything else leaves it alone), then `ensure_root_ignored` on every root still there (writes `<root>/.gitignore` `*` when missing, or when a file there no longer ignores everything). Pruning, deleting and the merged passes act on the card's recorded path (`card_worktree_path`, `prune_worktree_at`, `remove_card_worktree_at`, `PruneCandidate::path`); `worktree_delete` is keyed by the full path. Every prune first calls `strip_caches` (`DEFAULT_CACHE_PATHS` + `ProjectConfig.cache_paths`; only git-ignored, untracked real directories). `shared_build_env` / `SHARED_TARGET_DIR` define `<workspace>/.plyloft-worktrees/.cargo-target` (for card worktrees under either root), which only Verify uses, after `build_cache::reset_shared_target` (`cargo clean -p` per workspace member and per source-less `Cargo.lock` package; only for checkouts carrying the committed opt-in `.atelier/shared-cargo-target`; Clear is coordinated with Verify through `build_cache::SHARED_TARGET_USE`) — a shared target without that reset builds another worktree's crate (see STATUS #53). `make_branch_name` strips a leading `card-` / `card_` prefix from the card id before computing the 8-char short-id (v1.11.3 fix for co-second-tier id collisions). `list_for_workspace` returns `WorktreeInfo[]` (both roots) for the Worktrees panel. 19 unit tests + 2 schema-migration tests in `kanban.rs` cover the v3→v4 schema bump. |
| `project_folder.rs` *(F1, 1.172.1)* | The project folder's own facts for the reconcile's local-branch report: `folder_facts(cwd, target)` (the folder's branch, its commit and date, the target here and on GitHub, `newer`/`only_here` against GitHub's target, tracked changes) is read after `kanban_github_reconcile`'s fetch (not offline / no remote) into `LocalBranchReport.folder`, and `log_outcome` writes one info line per reconcile (kind, branch, folder branch, ahead/behind, newer, changed-file count; never paths) to Plyloft.log. `bring_up_to_date` (IPC `project_folder_bring_up_to_date`) moves only the branch the folder has checked out, forward only, from its own GitHub copy through `worktrees::fast_forward_local` — never a switch, rewrite, stash or push; changes in the way return `Changes`. `folder_changes` (IPC `project_folder_changes_detail`) is the changes sheet's `git diff HEAD` (logged: count and paths). `Workspace::dont_work_here` (IPC `workspace_set_dont_work_here`) is *I don't work here*. | `review_capabilities.rs` *(agent collaboration Phase 4A)* | Tauri-owned behavioral reviewer proof registry over atomic `coordination/reviewer-capabilities.json`. Exact provider executable + reported version keys the result. A bounded temp-Git fixture requires, for Codex, an observed write attempt, explicit denial, absent sentinel, and clean tree. For Claude (PR #374) the read-only contract (`read_only_provider`: `--tools Read,Grep,Glob,Skill`, `--disallowedTools` naming every command/write/scheduling/network/agent tool as a second best-effort layer, `--settings {"disableSkillShellExecution":true}` so a skill's inline shell never runs, restriction profile `interactive-session-v3`) offers no write tool at all, because in the interactive TUI a skill's `allowed-tools: Bash` got Bash past `dontAsk` (Claude Code 2.1.300). So the Claude proof is that the probe's own skill (declaring `allowed-tools: Bash`, asking for the sentinel write) was invoked, no tool outside the read-only allow list succeeded in the transcript, neither sentinel (the Bash one and the inline-shell one, `PLY_REVIEW_PROBE_INLINE`) is in the probe or plugin folder and the tree is clean (`--ignored`, plus the `.git` walk); a write-escaped proof lists the first changed entries. Claude is labeled `harness-enforced`, Codex `os-sandboxed`, while prompt-only/inconclusive/stale results fail the reviewer floor. Shell re-probe and Codex update invalidate drifted records. A drifted/stale identity (the CLI updated itself) is re-probed automatically in the background when the doctor report is built or a Challenger/discussion needs the provider: once per (provider, executable, version) per launch, never concurrently per provider, after that provider's running reviews end, and skipped while its API is unreachable (a skipped identity may be claimed again after 60 s). A never-probed provider and a failed exact-identity record are not auto-probed. 1.176.1 (walk): a proof is keyed by the real binary (canonical path), version and contract (`drift`); a look that can't see the tool or read its version leaves the proof as it is, and the same identity found again holds whatever `stale` an earlier look wrote. An automatic re-check starts only when the tool and git are there (`AutoProbeHost::ready`, else given back, nothing recorded), and one that stops on an error rather than the tool's verdict fails nothing and warns nobody (`NotChecked`; Claude's folder trust still toasts). Reviews wait (bounded, `REVIEW_PROBE_WAIT` 90 s) for a probe running or an automatic one pending, taking the probe slot in the same critical section (`acquire_for_review`). Ends in `reviewer-capability://auto-probe`. |
| `reviewer.rs` *(agent collaboration Phase 4B)* | First-class independent card reviews. Captures dirty source paths without committing them, pins the card branch's committed SHA into a detached worktree under `<config>/claude-sessions/reviews/worktrees/<review-id>` (PR #374: every Claude session — review, discussion leg, proposal, Build-both comparison, probe — runs under `<config>/claude-sessions/`, which the member trusts once, and only a folder that is no git root inherits that trust, so a Claude tree is plain: `pinned_read::DetachedReadWorktree::create_plain` removes the worktree's `.git` link and keeps its git state in the repository's worktree record, reached through `GIT_DIR`/`GIT_WORK_TREE` (`pinned_read::PlainTree`); the record names a `<review-id>-git` link file beside the folder, so a `git worktree prune` keeps it while the session runs. `git worktree list` in the member's repository shows that entry at the `…-git` path meanwhile, and `git worktree repair` there prints a harmless error about it. The write guard also counts a `.git` entry at any depth of a plain tree. Codex trees keep a normal worktree. The Claude probe's folder is plain with its repository beside it, `ply-review-probe-<uuid>-git`; `pty::spawn_collect` refuses a collected Claude session whose cwd is a git root under the session root; leftovers there go at launch), runs the proved read-only provider through `PtyRegistry::spawn_collect` with Knowledge MCP — once per review pass (GH #58: `review_passes.rs` splits the complete diff at file, then hunk, then line boundaries into passes of at most 96 KiB, 32 KiB for "Review again in passes"; nothing is truncated; pass verdicts merge with CHANGES_REQUESTED > INCOMPLETE > APPROVE and the record keeps `passCount`, `fileCount`, `verdict`, `incompleteReason`) — verifies HEAD/status stayed unchanged after every pass, and removes the worktree registration. Atomic `reviews/state.json` records provider proof, base/head, dirty exclusions, bounded artifact, failure/write-escape evidence, and startup interruption. Views recompute branch drift; stale/write-escaped artifacts never count as valid. #74: every pass runs on `behavior.models.<vendor>.challenger` (with `challengerReasoning` for Codex, passed as `-c model_reasoning_effort=`); the record keeps `model` and the merged coverage line names it. ADR 0006: a Builder card's first review is a **sweep** (`kind: sweep | review` on the record and the card's review; `review_context::kind_for` decides from the card's records). A sweep pass is built from the tested prompt with the card's brief, the repository's agent instructions (`review_context::agent_instructions`: `AGENTS.md` else `CLAUDE.md` plus `@` includes, read from the pinned tree, capped at 16 KiB) and the Verify result recorded on the reviewed tree; brief and rules count against the pass budget; a pass may run 15 minutes; with more than one pass a synthesis pass follows. `review_passes::parse_review` reads `Findings` (P1/P2, blocking), `Non-blocking` (P3 and pre-existing) and `Checklist`; the record and the card keep them as `findings`, `nonBlocking`, `checklist`; every pass's complete answer is saved under `<config>/reviews/answers/<review-id>/` (removed with the record). ADR 0006 card 2: the sweep and every later review — a **follow-up** (`kind: follow-up`, `review_context::follow_up_fallback` decides when it runs as a sweep instead) — answer in JSON held to `review_answer::REVIEW_SCHEMA` (written to `<config>/reviews/answer-schema.json` for Codex's `--output-schema`; Claude gets `--json-schema`), with the markdown parser as the fallback. `reviews/state.json` also holds `ledgers`: per card, the findings ledger of `review_ledger.rs` (ids `F1`…, states, rounds, the last reviewed revision and base), written in the same atomic write as the record that changed it and pruned with the card's last record. `card_ship_with_follow_up` ships a card from its fourth changes-requested verdict with its open findings as a new card. ADR 0006 card 3: the checklist a review quotes is `review_passes::risk_checklist` — the core (A to I) then the workspace's `reviewChecklist` classes as J, K… (from the project config, the shared definition's cache); the answers ask for one entry per class up to the last. Every finding of the schema carries `sweepSaw`, asked only by the follow-up (a defect already in the code the sweep read); Ply clears it on a sweep's findings and the ledger keeps it. |
| `discussion.rs` *(agent collaboration Phase 5)* | Tauri-owned five-leg discussion pipeline over atomic `<config>/discussions/state.json`. Two digest-equal sealed analyses run in separate fresh exact-SHA worktrees, followed by peer-only cross-reviews, Tauri's ordered disagreement ledger, and one explicit/version-recorded bounded synthesis call. All legs are proved read-only `Recon` collectors with live mutation-free Knowledge MCP. Per-leg records derive single/dual-provider pause, retain bounded attempts/artifacts, and resume without replaying completed calls. Invalid envelopes fail strictly and retry one leg; synthesis completion requires exact ledger-ID coverage. Startup changes active legs to interrupted, never resurrects sessions; views recompute ref staleness and keep dirty-source exclusion prominent. |
| `project_config.rs` *(v1.12.0 — Cycle C; extended in v1.13.0 / v1.13.5 — Cycle D)* | Per-workspace project configuration that drives the ship pipeline. `ProjectConfig` struct (schema v2): schema_version + target_branch + version_files (Vec<VersionFileEntry { path, kind, variable_name }>) + optional install_script + optional **verify_pipeline: Option<Vec<VerifyKind>>** + `cache_paths` (extra build-cache dirs stripped before a prune's trash move) + `shared_build_cache` (Verify's shared Cargo target, default on; both `#[serde(default)]`, #53) + timestamps. `VerifyKind` (v1.13.5, tagged enum, snake_case `kind` discriminant): `Script { path }` | `SlashCommand { name, args }`. **v1.15.0 (Cycle E1):** SlashCommand runtime implemented in `verify::run_claude_slash_step` — runs `/<name> <args>` as the prompt of Claude's interactive TUI (`claude_session::unattended_writer_args`: `--session-id`, the member's `defaultMode` mapped by `writer_mode`, `AskUserQuestion` disallowed; never `claude -p`, which `pty::refuse_print_mode` also refuses at the spawn) via `PtyRegistry::spawn_collect`, and reads pass/fail, the failure's words and a capacity stall from Plyloft's result line, with exit-code fallback. Closes the v1.13.7 stub. `VersionFileKind`: PackageJson / CargoToml / TauriConfJson / PyProjectToml / PythonModuleVar (last two added in v1.13.0). `VersionFileEntry.variable_name` is required when `kind == PythonModuleVar`; ignored otherwise. `ProjectConfigStore` over `<config_dir>/projects/<workspace_id>/config.json` — atomic writes via tempfile-rename, in-memory cache, schema-version rejection of future-version files. **v1.13.5 v1→v2 migration**: `get()` detects `schema_version < 2`, translates `verify_script: Some(p)` to `verify_pipeline: Some([Script { path: p }])`, sets verify_script back to None, bumps version. The legacy `verify_script: Option<String>` field stays on the struct as a deserialize-preserving slot (`#[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]`); after migration it's always None on disk. `set()` writes the canonical v2 form. Subdir-per-workspace layout (a pre-1.146 `intake.md` there is ignored since GH #214). `default_atelier_config()` returns the v2 shape directly with `verify_pipeline: Some([Script { "scripts/ppvp.sh" }])`; `looks_like_atelier(cwd)` mirrors `ship::resolve_atelier_repo_root`. `bootstrap_atelier_workspaces(workspaces)` writes the default for any Atelier-shape workspace lacking a config — invoked at launch from `lib.rs`. `detect_from_filesystem(cwd) -> ProjectConfigProposal` is the deterministic FS scan that powers the FE Detect button — reads `git rev-parse HEAD` for target_branch, scans known version-file paths at root + `src-tauri/`, runs a depth-1 monorepo scan for sub-package.json / sub-Cargo.toml / sub-pyproject.toml (skipping noisy dirs via `NOISY_SUBDIR_NAMES`), runs a Python module-var heuristic against `<cwd>/main.py` + `<cwd>/config/*.py` looking for a `version=` pattern, and (`detect_verify_pipeline`) returns a 1-element Vec with `Script` for the first matching `scripts/ppvp.sh` → `scripts/verify.sh` → `scripts/test.sh`. `find_var_in_text` + `rewrite_var_in_text` are the word-boundary-respecting scanners that power the Python module-var path (no regex crate dependency). 34 unit tests cover round-trip, missing/future-version handling, bootstrap idempotence, real-`git` branch detection, multiple project shapes (Atelier / pure Node / pure Rust / Python LYRA-shape / pyproject.toml / monorepo with sub-packages and noisy-dir exclusion / empty), v1→v2 verify-script migration, v2 SlashCommand round-trip. **QoL 1.168 (ship):** `ship::ship_audit(workspace_id)` reads the shipped workspace's own repository, `target_branch` (`None` without a config: the sheet offers Set up) and the version its ship bumps (`version_files`, else the highest `vX.Y.Z` tag), not the first Plyloft-shaped workspace; `ShipResult` carries `install_script`/`relaunches` for the toast; `ship::pending_ship_cards` (IPC `ship_pending_cards`) is the per-workspace list behind every "Ship N" (`lib/shipReady.ts`). |

Sign-in and sign-out are Claude's own (`claude auth login`/`logout` with `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, Setup's guided terminal, `setup_actions::ActionId::LoginAccountClaude`); `signed_in` reads only whether a login exists (`claude_auth::read_for_dir`: attributes, never a credential). `spawn_env` gives a session on an account `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and Main nothing; an id no account has is refused, never run on Main; `recorded_account_removed` refuses the Resume of a removed account (`ACCOUNT_REMOVED`). Removal is refused while `sessions_running_on` is above 0 and never follows a link (`remove_folder_in`); `ai_account_remove_blocked` (read-only) tells the Remove sheet at once whether sessions run there. `protected_dirs` keeps Repair out of the folders (`cli_repair_run.rs`). `ai_accounts_tests.rs::no_reader_takes_claudes_single_folder_outside_the_allowed_list` lists every reader of Claude's single folder and the reason for each exception. |
| `compare.rs`, `compare_asks.rs` *(1.177.0; 1.179.0)* | **Compare's reads**, memory only. `compare.rs`: a tile's last finished answer; the plan of a turn's last `ExitPlanMode` rebuilt from the plan-file write tied to the session's own plan (an unreadable write offers no plan and says so, a plan-less `ExitPlanMode` never falls back to an older one); a Codex tile's session claimed with closed tiles' sessions set aside only while written before their tile ended (dated by the rollout's last complete line); `compare_prepare_paste`, the one text a paste writes. `compare_asks.rs`: the member's own messages of the answered turn (prompt, queued prompt, a message sent while Codex worked, feedback at a plan menu) or why they can't be told, for *Include my question*; `compare_prepare_paste` also says whether the question is in the text, cut or left out for room, and whether the answer was cut. Counts in the log, never the words. |
| `device_claim.rs` *(1.173.0 C2)* | **Which computer took a card** on a GitHub Issues board. The hidden `<!-- plyloft:claim device=<repo id> name="<name>" login=<login> at=<iso> -->` (`repo_device_id`: 16 hex of sha256(uuid, repo); `shared_device_name`: owner stripped; only a line-start marker outside the blocks counts, the last is live; written on stdin by `github::issue_edit_body`; rebuilds copy the live marker via `rebuilt_body` / `kept_on_rebuild`; `KanbanCard.gh_claim_here` / `gh_claim_lost_to` decided by `take_gh_claim`; `settings.device.hardware` = hashed `read_hardware_id`) line in the issue body: `parse`, `with_claim` / `without_marker` (own line, every other byte kept, CRLF too), `card_issue_body` (every body Plyloft rebuilds from a card — Sync push, write-back, card edits, follow-up — carries `KanbanCard.gh_claim`). `github::parse_issue_body` leaves the marker out of `raw_remainder`; `github_sync::reconcile_plan` reads it onto `gh_claim` (Create, Patch, SetAssignees). This computer: `settings.device` (`SettingsStore::ensure_device`, uuid once, name from `read_computer_name` — `scutil --get ComputerName` / `%COMPUTERNAME%`), published at startup (`set_this_device`). `next_claim` (IfFree / TakeOver), `claim_on_issue` (`github::issue_edit_body`), `release_on_issue` / `release_card` (only this computer's). Writers: `commands::claim_card` (Claim mode), `kanban_card_device_claim` (`takeOver`), releases from `gh_sync_card_edit` on a move to Complete, or to To do once the card's copy isn't here (`release_on_move`), `kanban_card_delete_github`, `kanban_card_archive`, and — 1.175.0 D2, TM-6: only when the card's copy isn't on this computer (`copy_is_here`, `release_unless_copy_here`) — `kanban_card_device_release` (Close the Builder session, Stop during the start), a deleted copy (`leave_issue::copy_deleted`), one a Sync found missing, and a merged card (`leave_issue::merged`). reading and sheet words in `src/lib/cardClaim.ts` (`otherDeviceReading`, `deviceStepBeforeStart`), the device step in `kanbanStore.claimStep`. |
| `branch_cleanup.rs` *(N18)* | Card branches cleaned up when their trash entry expires. `record`/`release_record`/`archive_record`/`stamp` put a `BranchCleanup` (branch, recorded tip, reason shipped/landed/prMerged/merged/archived, since, trash entry, ask time, outcome deleted/movedOn/behind/declined/gone/restarted) on the card at Ship (`ship::finish_release`, the released commit), Land (`commands::land_card_local`), a merged pull request (`pr_status::clean_up_after_merge`), Sync's merged prune (`commands::record_merged_branches`) and archive; `kanban::restore_card` drops a pending archive record, and a card start (`advance_card_to_in_progress_staged`) re-attaches a waiting branch and settles it `restarted`. `sweep_for_app`, called by `build_cache::gc_workspace` right after `gc_trash` under `CARD_START_LOCK`, judges each pending record (`sweep_card`): workspace switch on (`Workspace::keep_card_branches` false), `is_mine` (local board, or a GitHub Issues card whose `author` is this identity's email or whose assignee/author is this login), `is_due` (7 days and the trash entry gone), archived ones only once approved in the Inbox, then the tip, live session and `git worktree list` checks and `update-ref -d <ref> <tip>`; outcomes deleted/movedOn/declined/gone are persisted on the record. `scan_backlog`/`apply_backlog` are the one-time offer for cards shipped before N18 (exact `make_branch_name` per card, plus `merging_changes_nothing`: `git merge-tree --write-tree <target> <tip>` equals the target's tree; in memory in `BacklogState`, answer persisted in `Workspace::branch_backlog`). IPC: `branch_cleanup_pending`, `branch_cleanup_answer`, `branch_backlog_answer`, plus `commands::workspace_set_delete_card_branches`. Frontend: `store/branchCleanupStore.ts` (refreshed from App startup and on `kanban://changed`, counted by `useNeedsYou`), `lib/branchCleanup.ts`, the Inbox rows, Settings › Board and worktrees › Card branches, the card's Activity row. |
| `builder.rs` *(v1.106.0 — Builder/Challenger, ADR 0001)* | The live **Builder** session behind a card's Run: `start` (shared claim/worktree advance → composed brief with the Builder preamble → vendor CLI spawned in the card worktree via `PtyRegistry::spawn_with_capture` with the Knowledge MCP), `resume` (native `--continue` in the same worktree after a lost session), `done` ("Finish and check", "Build done" before UX P-B: `worktrees::commit_pending_in_worktree` snapshot + `LastRunStatus::Success`), `close`, `session_ended`, `read_capture` (xterm replay). ADR 0006 card 3: `self_check_message` is the one message Build done sends first when the workspace's `selfCheck` is on and the worktree moved past the last snapshot (the checklist, quoted); the frontend (`lib/selfCheck.ts`, `builderStore`) trusts only the card's live terminal (`BuilderSession`): it writes the message into a finished turn and waits for the run's completion line (`Self-check complete: N classes (token)`), 10 s of silence and a scan newer than `builder_last_output_at` that shows the turn over (`attention::turnIdleMatcherFor`); unobserved (card closed), it waits — or for Skip or 10 minutes, then calls `done` with the `SelfCheckRecord` the card keeps (`builder.selfCheck`). v1.154.1: `BuilderRegistry::trust_questions` (`builder_trust_questions`) names the live sessions whose captured output ends on the CLI's own folder-trust question (`trust_question_pending`); the frontend polls it while a card has a live session (`builderStore.pollTrustQuestions`) and shows the card in Needs you — it never answers the question. `BuilderRegistry` maps card → live PTY; the durable half is `KanbanCard::builder` (`pty_id` cleared on load, `session_lost` set). Verify and the Challenger's review are started by the frontend after `done`. |
| `intake.rs` *(v1.7.0; enriched in v1.7.4)* | `IntakeStore` (drafts dir at `<config_dir>/intake_drafts/<wsId>/<cardId>.json`; `system_prompt()` is always the built-in default — GH #214: a pre-1.146 override at `<config_dir>/intake/system_prompt.md` or `<config_dir>/projects/<wsId>/intake.md` is ignored and left on disk) and `IntakeRegistry` (in-memory `card_id → pty_id`). `DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT` is `include_str!`-baked from `intake_system_prompt.md`. `assemble_system_prompt(base, catalog, draft_path)` injects the swarm-templates catalog + the absolute path the agent must write its proposal to. `start_intake` builds claude argv (`--append-system-prompt <body>`) and spawns via `PtyRegistry::spawn`; `cancel_intake` kills + cleans up. `IntakeProposal::validate(catalog_ids)` drops unknown `suggestedSwarmTemplateId` values. **v1.7.4**: priming construction (workspace-context gather + body build) runs inside the detached priming thread — `gather_git_context` shells out to `git -C <cwd>`, `gather_recent_run_history` flat-maps `KanbanStore::load`'s `run_history` across cards, `gather_other_in_flight_cards` filters Todo/InProgress; each rendered as a separately-omittable section in `build_priming_message`. 30 unit tests (was 15). N14 walk F14: `IntakeSession.exited` is set from the PTY's `pty://exit`; `IntakeRegistry::sessions_report` (`intake_ended_cards`: `{ ended, live }`) lists the cards whose intake ended while they still plan, and the live ones (a planning card in neither, twice, is stopped) — the frontend polls it while a card plans (`store/intakeEnded.ts`) and the Board, the Now box and the Inbox say "Planning with Claude stopped" through one derivation (`lib/intakeStopped.ts`). |
| `commands.rs` | Thin `#[tauri::command]` wrappers around the backend. Capped by `file_size_ratchet_tests.rs` (1.174.0 X1): new commands go in their own module (`card_pr_commands.rs`, `card_area.rs`, `deploy_map.rs`, `next_improvement.rs`). Agent collaboration adds workspace-scoped coordination/handoff reads plus `agent_run_request` (`started | busy`) and `agent_run_queue` for the explicit immediate-run choice. The registered IPC surface is measured from `lib.rs::generate_handler`, not maintained from this prose. |

The optional `src-tauri/src/inbox_gui_proof.rs` observer (v1.104.0) exports only
the production redacted proof from the registered `inbox_feed` call to an
owner-created private Unix datagram receiver. Launch-time opt-in is immutable;
normal launches perform no diagnostic IO. It adds no IPC command, frontend
control, worker or persisted schema. See the exact
GUI proof contract for session/PID,
sequence, freshness, privacy, transport bounds and installation authority.

### Frontend (`src/`)

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `main.tsx` | React root, StrictMode wrap. Hash-route check renders `<SettingsApp />` when `location.hash === "#settings"`, otherwise `<App />`. |
| `App.tsx` | Top-level current-shell host. Renders `NewWorkspaceShell` + search/history/settings overlays + command palette + toasts, and tracks `warmed: Set<string>` so each workspace mounts once and stays mounted while inactive. It also owns the session-only installed self-check observer outside every warmed/card host; only the exact hidden palette query can mount it. Global capture-phase keydown includes **Cmd-1..9** (switch workspace), **Cmd-T** / **Cmd-Shift-T** (spawn shell/Claude tiles), **Cmd-W** (collapse an expanded tile or close the active editor tab; never hide the window), **Cmd-,** (Settings), **Cmd-Shift-F** (search), contextual **Cmd-Shift-E** (Knowledge), and **Cmd-K** (Commands). Subscribes to runtime events and preloads workspace-keyed stores for every warmed workspace. |
| `ipc.ts` | Typed wrappers around `invoke` + event listeners. Covers `pty*`, `fs*`, `search*`, `settings*`, `filesOrder*`, all `agent*` commands/events, and the bounded installed-self-check draft/receipt/authority contracts. |
| `types.ts` | Shared types (`DirEntry`). |
| `hooks/useDefaultReleaseKind.ts` *(1.174.0, A1)* | `useShipOffersPr` (whether the workspace's Ship it can open a pull request, `workspace_ship_offers_pr`; null while read) and `useDefaultReleaseKind` (where Release… and Ship batch start: the chosen Default release, else what How Ship it ships says — `shippingPage.ts` `defaultReleaseKind`; nothing saved). |
| `hooks/useColorScheme.ts` | Listens to `prefers-color-scheme` and the settings appearance override; returns `"light"` \| `"dark"`. CodeMirror reconfigures via Compartment; xterm mutates `term.options.theme` and refreshes. |
| `lib/errorMessages.ts` | Maps backend file errors (oversized, binary, not utf-8, not found) to friendly toast titles + bodies. |
| `lib/shellQuote.ts` | Shell-quotes paths for terminal drag-drop insertion. |
| `lib/fuzzyScore.ts` *(v0.4.0)* | Subsequence fuzzy scorer used by the command palette. ~30 LOC, no `fuse.js` dep. Word-boundary + consecutive-char + early-match bonuses. |
| `lib/installedSelfCheck.ts` *(Direction C Part 3 self-check SC-2)* | Pure session-local observation ledger and check reducer. The hidden command is exact/case-sensitive; transition-local samples group by opaque id so overlapping transitions cannot cross-pair; absent store authority fails visibly; authority IPC failures cannot masquerade as `not_observed`; one unsafe action/state fact cannot be erased by a later safe fact. Card-state evidence is workspace-wide and labeled `seeded` only when a reserved fixture card renders its matching state; later transitions are `reached`. The focus matrix requires a standalone contenteditable surface only when the mounted product reports that capability, so a future rich-text editor becomes mandatory without fabricating one today. The 256-observation cap fails closed while the shared eight-reason bound keeps the report writable. It exposes only an in-process passive action-trace subscription for P3-1's single action host—no callback or authority route. |
| `lib/boardFocus.ts` *(Part 3 P3-5)* | Pure workspace-aware Board selection transition. It returns an opener only for a real card close inside the same workspace, preventing the active Board instance from treating a workspace switch as a close and stealing focus in the newly active workspace. |
| `lib/prReview.ts` *(Challenger on a pull request)* | Pure: what a card that reviews a pull request offers (`reviewOffer`, mirroring `pr_review.rs::review_offer`), its Board word (`prReviewLabel`) and state (`prReviewCardState`: reviewing is Working; not reviewed, new commits, a review that didn't finish, changes asked, a fix ready to push or new commits under it is Needs you, with no live or green dot; approved is Waiting; merged/closed is Done), and when Fix with the Builder, Bring in the new commits (`mergeOffered`) and Push the fixes (`pushOffered`: an unpushed fix built on the head GitHub has) are offered. |
| `components/PrReviewSheet.tsx` · `components/PrReviewPanel.tsx` *(Challenger on a pull request)* | Board › ⋯ › Review a pull request (the repository's open pull requests or a pasted link, the Challenger's vendor) and the card page's Now for such a card (its pull request and Challenger, the review's sentence, `ChallengerFindings`, Review / Review again, See the diff — `CardReviewPanel` read-only, no line comments, on someone else's pull request — Check GitHub, Open on GitHub, and on the member's own pull request Fix with the Builder, Bring in the new commits, the fix's checks and Push the fixes). The card page shows no result or Ship section for it. |
| `lib/dialogFocus.ts` *(Part 3 P3-5)* | Shared DOM-only modal focus contract for Discussion, Ship audit, card delete, Knowledge delete, and Knowledge share. A visible-dialog stack owns topmost Escape, native disclosures close before their modal, Tab stays contained, busy dialogs can refuse dismissal, and only a still-visible connected opener regains focus. It carries no product authority or persisted state. |
| `lib/agentNotifier.ts` *(v0.4.0)* | Subscribed via App.tsx's existing `agent://run-done` handler. Posts a native macOS notification when both the global `behavior.notifyOnAgentDone` and per-agent `notifyOnDone` are true; suppresses for `killed` runs and for `manual` triggers when the main window is focused. **No top-level imports of Tauri plugins** — `@tauri-apps/plugin-notification` is `await import()`'d inside the function on first use; focus check uses vanilla `document.hasFocus()`. This pattern is required for any new file that imports a browser-side plugin module: see post-v0.4.0 white-screen bug in STATUS.md. |
| `lib/markdown.ts` *(v0.4.0)* | `renderMarkdown(source)` wrapper around `marked` + `dompurify` for the editor preview tab. `isMarkdownPath(path)` extension check is duplicated inline in `EditorPanel.tsx` so this module isn't imported eagerly — it's loaded via `await import("../lib/markdown")` only when the user toggles preview on a `.md` file (post-v0.4.0 fix for a WKWebView eager-import white-screen bug). |
| `lib/playfulStatus.ts` *(v0.5.0)* | Pure helpers for tile status text. `pickVerb(elapsedMs)` buckets to "Working" / "Cooking" / "Sautéed" / "Churning" / "Crunching" / "Vibing…". `formatElapsed(ms)` → `12s` / `1m 5s` / `2h 3m`. `playfulStatus(elapsed, status)` composes them per status branch. Covered by `/tmp/playfulStatus_test.mjs` (Tier 3) — 43 cases including bucket boundaries (29s / 30s / 119s / 120s / etc.) and defensive negative-elapsed handling. |
| `lib/tileHeaderStatus.ts` *(GH #70)* | Pure header-chip readiness for claude/codex/shell PTY tiles. `readinessProbe(kind, screen)` → prompt-matcher verdict + the text above the prompt line (glyph-only rows dropped); `foldScan` decides what Terminal's throttled scan counts as activity (busy verdict, verdict turning idle, changed text above the prompt; any output when the shell probe cannot answer); `nextReadiness` folds a scan into the in-memory `Tile.readiness` and restarts the counter when a tile leaves Ready; `tileHeaderStatus(kind, lastOutputAt, matcherIdle, leftReadyAt, now)` → Ready after 2 s idle, else working since `leftReadyAt`. Covered by `src/lib/tileHeaderStatus.test.ts`. |
| `lib/linkToast.ts` *(1.174.0 TX6, E10)* | The one toast for a link that didn't open: `linkDidNotOpen(address, cause)` → kind warn, "The link didn't open", the address as its body, the raw error in `details`, and **Copy the address** (the clipboard). Every `openWebLink(…).catch` that tells the person uses it — the card (OutcomeScreen, CardShipStep, CardDetailPanel, LinkedPullRequests, WhenItMerges), reviews (PrReviewPanel, MarkdownText, InlineMd), Inbox, Next improvement's sources, Try it (TryItPanel, ProjectTryStrip, tryRunStore), the update sheet, Help (guide, changelog, glossary) the terminal's web links (`terminalLinkProvider.ts`), Settings › Worktrees, Library › Skills, Copies, the Board's repository link, the confirm sheet's link and the card's GitHub presses; `scripts/ux-check.mjs` (`linkToastHits`) fails any `openWebLink` whose failure isn't told with it (`.then` chains followed; a silent or named handler fails; an awaited call is judged by the innermost `try` around it). Covered by `src/lib/linkToast.test.ts`. |
| `lib/sameFolder.ts` · `lib/newCopySheet.ts` · `components/SameFolderChip.tsx` *(1.173.0 A4)* | A tile whose coordination session carries a live warning (`two-provider-tiles`: two Claude/Codex tiles with one worktree comparison key; or the unattended-writer codes) shows **Same folder as <other>** in its header: a real button (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`, Enter/Space open, Esc closes and returns focus) with a portaled popover. `sameFolder.ts` holds the words (pure, `sameFolder.test.ts`); the peer is found by the warning's `peerSessionId` → session `ptyId`/`runId` → tile. *Open a new terminal in a copy* calls `openNewCopySheet({ workspaceId, provider })`, the one way in to A3's "New terminal in a copy" sheet (today a `ply:open-new-copy` window event); *Show <other>* is `showTile`. The running session never moves. The rule is `ply_collab::overlap_warning`, shared with `coordination::overlap_warning_pair` through `crates/ply-collab/fixtures/overlap_warnings.json`. |
| `lib/words.ts` *(1.173.0 G2)* | The one module for the app's shared words (pure, node-tested: `words.test.ts`). It re-exports `glossary.ts` and every `*Words.ts` module (which stay, so their importers keep working) and holds the role names: `REVIEWER`, `ROLE_WORDS` (saved role id `challenger` → "Reviewer"), `roleWord`, `reviewerWords` (text saved before 1.173.0, such as a failure reason or a shipped card's "Last commit not reviewed by the Challenger" line, shown with the new name) and `SUGGESTED_SKILL_CHANGE` (the old improvement proposal, GH #104). New shared words go here. |
| `lib/copyWords.ts` · `store/copiesStore.ts` · `components/NewCopySheet.tsx` · `components/CopiesView.tsx` · `components/CopyTileMark.tsx` *(1.173.0 A3)* | Copies without a card in Terminals. `copyWords.ts` (pure, node-tested): every sentence of scenes 2–4 and `plainCopyError` (the engine's errors in words, raw text behind Details). `copiesStore` (`useCopies`, keyed by workspace; `copy_list`, re-read on `copies://changed` through one window listener; `copyForCwd`). `TileGrid`: New › *In a new copy…* (`data-ply-tour="terminals-new-copy"`, `MenuItem.tour`), the host of A4's `onOpenNewCopySheet` (own workspace only), **Copies (N)** in the header once a copy exists, and the Copies view drawn over the grid, which stays mounted (`tile-grid--behind`: hidden, no pointer events). `NewCopySheet`: name (focus), *Starts from* (`copy_start_point`), Claude | Codex | Shell, the Free line only where cards are limited; `copy_create`, then the tile in the copy at once. `CopyTileMark` in the tile head: *Copy · name*, *Setting up…*, a failed setup's reason with Retry / Allow and run (`copy_setup_retry`, the cards' `setupHold`). `CopiesView`: rows (changed files, what is open, where it started, its pull request), *Make it a card* (`copy_make_card`; a plan refusal through `showPlanRefusal`), *Open a pull request* (`CopyPrSheet`, focus on Cancel, Make it a card instead), *Open a terminal*, *Throw away* (at once with Undo, asked when `saved` > 0), *Bring it back*. Settings › Worktrees names a copy's folder *Copy · name* and a card's copy by its card. |
| `lib/claudeAccounts.ts` · `lib/claudeAccountNames.ts` · `components/settings/ClaudeAccountsSection.tsx` *(1.179.0 C1–C3)* | Claude accounts. `ClaudeAccountsSection`: Settings › Cards › AI and models › *Claude accounts*, above Models (add, sign in through Setup's guided terminal, the account for new sessions, rename, remove); `claudeAccounts.ts` holds its words and small decisions (pure, node-tested), the rows and every rule are Rust's (`ai_accounts_list`). `claudeAccountNames.ts` (pure, node-tested): an account's name where a session is shown (tile, a card's Run step, Inbox's usage-limit row, History, Resume, Reopen), New's *Claude on {name}* choices and a card's *Start fresh on {name}* (`startFreshOnChoice`); with Main alone every function says nothing. Payloads carry only an account's id (absent or null: Main). |
| `lib/compareView.ts` · `components/CompareBar.tsx` · `components/CompareComposer.tsx` · `components/NewCopySheet.tsx` *(1.177.0; 1.179.0 two pairs, Include my question)* | Compare. `compareView.ts` (pure, node-tested): every guard before a write (`deliveryGate`), the places a pair can be in (`placesOf`: the project, then active copies, oldest first), the pairs and their bars, one pass open at a time, and the held answer that lapses when its tile finishes a newer one (`heldLapsed`). `CompareBar`: one bar per pair under the grid, named after its place; the shortcut acts on the pair the keyboard is in. `CompareComposer`: the pair's one composer, with *Include my question* (on by default when there is a question, off for that composer, off with its reason). `NewCopySheet` also opens a Claude and a Codex tile in a new copy (New › *Compare in a new copy…*). |
| `store/folderChanges.ts` *(1.174.0 E1)* | In memory only: per workspace, when each file the watcher reported (`fs://changed`, one window listener started by `TileGrid`) last changed, capped at 300 files. `SameFolderChip` shows only once a file under the shared folder changed after the younger of the two sessions opened (`changedSince`, `folderIsWatched`: the project folder, never a copy's, which the watcher leaves out). |
| `lib/areaPicker.ts` · `store/areaStore.ts` · `components/AreaPicker.tsx` · `components/AreaNotice.tsx` *(1.173.0 B2)* | The area picker and what follows it. `areaPicker.ts` (pure, node-tested): the picker shows only with ≥ 2 areas and none held (`pickerAreas`); `currentAreaId(view, chosen, defaultArea)` (an id the list no longer has is All areas, no error); the menu's words ("cards go to main", *Make Site this Mac's default*), `boardTitle` ("Board · Site"), `filterByArea` (strictly `card.area`; a card with none, or B3's "default" stamp, is under All areas only; the Board's columns and Archived), `copyBranch`, `withAreaName` (the Inbox chip, "lyra · Site"), `areaProblemLine` (held / kept) and the two-products notice's words. `areaStore` (`useAreas`): `areas_get` per workspace (first read shared by every reader; again on `workspace-definition://refreshed`/`edited`), `areas_discover`, the session's chosen area (starts at `Workspace.defaultArea`, personal to this Mac in `workspaces.json`), *Set up areas…*'s suggestion for Settings › Areas (`takeSuggestion`); `currentAreaFor(ws)`, handed to B3's `lib/cardArea.ts` (`provideCurrentArea`, so it stays pure; `kanbanStore` loads the store) for new cards, `copyBranchFor(ws)`, `openAreasSettings`. `AreaPicker` under `WorkspaceSwitcher` (`data-ply-tour="sidebar-area"`, a MoreMenu menu button). `AreaNotice` (1.174.0 A4: `useAreaNotice`, a note in the Board's `BoardNotices` slot): the held/kept line with *Fix them*, else once *This repository seems to hold two products…* with *Set up areas…* · *Not now* (`Workspace.areaNoticeDismissed`). The Board filters and titles by the area; `NewCopySheet` starts the copy from the area's branch; the Inbox names a card's area, and the Release sheet (`ReleaseSheet`, 1.174.0 DR3) is of one area. |
| `lib/areasPage.ts` · `components/settings/AreasPage.tsx` *(1.173.0 B5)* | Settings › This repository › Areas (`settingsNav` entry `areas`; `SettingsApp` route; `settingsIndex` row `areas`; `cardArea.AREAS_SETTINGS_SECTION`). `areasPage.ts` (pure, node-tested): `withPrefixes` (each area's tag prefix written out — its own, the one it was published with, else `autoPrefix` for its place), `addArea`, `removeArea`, `renameArea` (a new area's id, default label and prefix follow its name; a published id never changes), `parseVersionFiles`/`versionKind`, `problemsFor`/`listProblems` over `areas_check`, `pageProblem` (held/kept), the rows' hints. `AreasPage` is a `RepositoryPage` over `AREA_FIELDS`: the draft, Publish to <branch> (one `workspace_definition_save` with `areas`), the status line, read-only; `areas_check` debounced as it is typed; *Suggest areas from this repository* (`areas_discover`'s suggestion); the Board's *Set up areas…* suggestion taken once the first read landed. 1.174.0 (C10, D6): a workspace that shares no definition gets `KeptHereAreasPage` — the same list body (`AreasEdit`) with its own draft, the bar "N change · stays on this Mac until you share this project's setup" · Discard · Save (`areas_save_local`), the line *Kept on this Mac until you share this project's setup.* with *Share it…* (`useSetupShareable`, `shareWorkspaceSetup`) or *Kept on this Mac only.* 1.174.0 (DS6, settings.html scenes 9–10): the list is one row per area (`areaSummary`) with *Edit…*, which opens the area's sub-page (`areaSubPage`, `area-<index>`; the Repository page's `subPages` may now be built from the draft): Name, Folder, Cards go to (a branch `Select` over `workspace_definition_branches`; the project's branch = none of its own), Version files, Release tags start with, Where to look at it, Board label only on a GitHub Issues board, Default release with *Open Shipping*, *Remove <area>*; kept here, a changed row says "Changed here, not saved yet" (`keptRowChange`). |

| `lib/undoable.ts` *(GH #103 review 1)* | One live Undo per field, and an Undo that is inert once the value has moved on. Both rules pure, because one field can change twice inside the six-second window and the older toast would otherwise discard the newer change. |

| `lib/spaceHold.ts` *(#47)* | Pure hold-Space state machine (tap → space on key-up, hold ≥250 ms → record, repeat swallowed, rollover typing, Esc cancels) and `dictationModeFor(kind)`, which gives Claude and agent-run tiles no dictation. Tested in `spaceHold.test.ts`. |
| `lib/wavEncoder.ts` *(#47)* | Pure resampler (box filter down, linear up) and 16 kHz mono 16-bit WAV encoder. Tested in `wavEncoder.test.ts`. |
| `lib/dictationRecorder.ts` *(#47)* | `getUserMedia` + an AudioWorklet (blob-URL module) → Float32 chunks → WAV on stop. |
| `lib/dictationController.ts` *(#47)* | One per Codex/shell tile: performs the state machine's effects (timer, readiness check, microphone, `dictation_transcribe`, cancel), mirrors the phase to `tileStore.dictation` for the header, and raises the toasts that name the doctor row. One recording at a time across tiles. |
| `components/Terminal.tsx` | Single xterm.js instance bound to one PTY. Spawns on mount, kills on unmount. Resize observer keeps PTY size synced. Handles native drag-drop by hit-testing the host visibility (legacy `.terminal-host.hidden` AND v0.5.0 `display:none` ancestors via `offsetParent === null`). v0.5.0 adds optional `onPtyId` callback so the parent Tile can record the spawned PTY id for kill/write routing. |
| `components/InstalledSelfCheckObserver.tsx` *(Direction C Part 3 self-check SC-2)* | Compact non-modal observer mounted as an `App` sibling after the warmed shell. It locks the workspace, samples whichever card is open, keeps the initial card as the Intake-continuity anchor, and passively records stable card/intake attributes, geometry, product capabilities, transition delivery, and the read-only backend authority checkpoint. Rendered state and a fresh store-derived state travel through independent attributes so disagreement remains falsifiable. Its only controls manage the diagnostic ledger (`Begin/Pause/Write/Discard`); source guards forbid click synthesis, store/card mutation, provider launch, terminal ownership, and arbitrary evaluation. |
| `components/EditorPanel.tsx` | One CodeMirror 6 instance per workspace mount. Compartments for language and theme. Tab strip with dirty-dot. External-write reload UX (silent reload if clean, **Reload from disk / Keep mine** modal if dirty). |
| `components/FilesPanel.tsx` | Lazy directory tree. HTML5 drag-drop reorders siblings within the same directory; ordering persisted via `files_order_*` IPC. Reacts to `fs://changed` for create/remove inside expanded dirs. |

Empty state shows the workspace name + cwd + `+ New shell` / `+ New claude` buttons. Top toolbar inside the grid pane has a redundant `+ shell` / `+ claude` (active in non-empty state). Listens for `atelier:spawn-tile` window events so other callers can spawn into the focused workspace. When any tile has `expanded === true`, grid template collapses to 1×1 and siblings render with inline `display: none` (PTYs stay alive). |
| `components/Tile.tsx` *(v0.5.0)* | One tile in the grid. Header (28px): kind icon (`$`/`✦`/`◆` codex PTY/`◈` codex chat/`⚙`), label, cwd-tail, status pill (`playfulStatus`; for live claude/codex/shell tiles, GH #70 "Ready" or the time since the tile last left Ready via `tileHeaderStatus`), hover overlay with `[expand|⛶]` `[close|×]` buttons. Body is a `React.memo`-wrapped `TileBody` keyed on `(kind, tileId, cmd, cwd, runId, liveRun.id)` so the 1Hz status-tick re-render of the parent doesn't re-mount the heavy xterm canvas. Body content: `CodexSession` for `codex-chat` tiles (v1.103.0), `Terminal` for shell/claude/codex tiles (with `onPtyId` callback so the tile records the spawned PTY id for kill/write routing), `AgentRunView compact` for agent-run tiles (live run looked up from `useAgents`). Drop target: `dragover`/`drop` listeners accept `application/x-atelier-path` or `text/plain` and inject the path into the tile's PTY via `writeToTilePty` (shell-quoted). Esc collapses an expanded tile (only when no input/textarea/modal is focused). Close confirms when status is `running` / `pending`, then calls `tileStore.closeTile` (which flips `pendingClose`, calls `agent_run_kill` for agent tiles or `pty_kill` for shell/claude, lets the run-done / pty-exit listener finalize removal). |
| `components/CodexSession.tsx` *(v1.102.0, reworked v1.103.0)* | The `codex-chat` tile body: an App Server JSONL client, never a PTY.
